// FUNC 180053ae0 __alloca_probe
unsigned __int64 __fastcall _alloca_probe()
{
  unsigned __int64 result; // rax
  char *v1; // r10
  char *StackLimit; // r11
  char v3; // [rsp+18h] [rbp+8h] BYREF

  v1 = &v3 - result;
  if ( (unsigned __int64)&v3 < result )
    v1 = nullptr;
  StackLimit = (char *)NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackLimit;
  if ( v1 < StackLimit )
  {
    LOWORD(v1) = (unsigned __int16)v1 & 0xF000;
    do
    {
      StackLimit -= 4096;
      *StackLimit = 0;
    }
    while ( v1 != StackLimit );
  }
  return result;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180053b30 ??2@YAPEAX_K@Z
void *__fastcall operator new(size_t Size)
{
  size_t i; // rbx
  void *result; // rax

  for ( i = Size; ; Size = i )
  {
    result = malloc(Size);
    if ( result != nullptr )
      break;
    if ( callnewh(i) == 0 )
    {
      if ( i != -1 )
        __scrt_throw_std_bad_alloc();
      sub_1800544CC();
    }
  }
  return result;
}

// FUNC 180053b7c ?configure_argv@__scrt_narrow_argv_policy@@SAHXZ
static errno_t __fastcall __scrt_narrow_argv_policy::configure_argv()
{
  _crt_argv_mode startup_argv_mode; // eax

  startup_argv_mode = get_startup_argv_mode();
  return configure_narrow_argv(startup_argv_mode);
}

// FUNC 180053b90 ?find_pe_section@@YAPEAU_IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER@@QEAE_K@Z
_IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER *__fastcall find_pe_section(unsigned __int8 *const a1, unsigned __int64 a2)
{
  __int64 v2; // r8
  unsigned __int8 *v3; // r9
  unsigned __int8 *v4; // r8
  unsigned __int64 v5; // rcx

  v2 = *((int *)a1 + 15);
  v3 = &a1[v2 + 24 + *(unsigned __int16 *)&a1[v2 + 20]];
  v4 = &v3[40 * *(unsigned __int16 *)&a1[v2 + 6]];
  while ( 1 )
  {
    if ( v3 == v4 )
      return nullptr;
    v5 = *((unsigned int *)v3 + 3);
    if ( a2 >= v5 && a2 < (unsigned int)(v5 + *((_DWORD *)v3 + 2)) )
      break;
    v3 += 40;
  }
  return (_IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER *)v3;
}

// FUNC 180053be4 ?is_potentially_valid_image_base@@YA_NQEAX@Z
bool __fastcall is_potentially_valid_image_base(char *a1)
{
  char *v1; // rax

  return a1 != nullptr
      && *(_WORD *)a1 == 23117
      && *(_DWORD *)(v1 = &a1[*((int *)a1 + 15)]) == 17744
      && *((_WORD *)v1 + 12) == 523;
}

// FUNC 180053c20 __scrt_acquire_startup_lock
char _scrt_acquire_startup_lock()
{
  PVOID StackBase; // rcx
  signed __int64 v1; // rax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    StackBase = NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackBase;
    while ( 1 )
    {
      v1 = _InterlockedCompareExchange64(&_scrt_native_startup_lock, (signed __int64)StackBase, 0);
      if ( v1 == 0 )
        break;
      if ( StackBase == (PVOID)v1 )
        return 1;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180053c5c __scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c
char _scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c()
{
  _crt_argv_mode startup_argv_mode; // eax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    _isa_available_init();
  }
  else
  {
    startup_argv_mode = get_startup_argv_mode();
    if ( configure_narrow_argv(startup_argv_mode) != 0 )
      return 0;
    initialize_narrow_environment();
  }
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180053c90 __scrt_dllmain_before_initialize_c
bool _scrt_dllmain_before_initialize_c()
{
  return (unsigned __int8)_scrt_initialize_onexit_tables(0) != 0;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180053ca8 __scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_attach
char _scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_attach()
{
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
    return 0;
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
  {
    _vcrt_thread_detach();
    return 0;
  }
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180053cd0 __scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_detach
char _scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_detach()
{
  _vcrt_thread_detach();
  _vcrt_thread_detach();
